（一）本轮PPI修复成色
2026年10月，PPI同比上涨5.7%，总量回升背后行业价格修复仍明显分化。以2025年7月为观察起点，
40个工业大类行业中，22个行业价格上涨，占比55%，上涨覆盖略超过半数；其余18个行业价格继续下降，
主要包括汽车、通用设备、专用设备，以及食品、服装、医药等中下游行业。
价格回升的幅度与历史跌幅的收复程度也存在差异，目前仍有30个行业的价格指数低于2022年10月水
平，占比75%。部分行业虽然已经反弹，但尚不足以弥补此前累积的跌幅，多数行业价格仍处于相对低位。

风险提示：国际大宗商品价格大幅波动；终端需求恢复不及预期；上游涨价向中下游传导不畅；行业权重及黄金贡献估算
存在偏差，实际价格走势偏离翘尾测算假设。

（三）CPI修复进入平台期
10月CPI和核心CPI同比分别上涨0.7%和0.8%，均较9月回落0.2个百分点。核心CPI涨幅回落约六成来
自黄金支撑减弱；剔除黄金后的超核心价格指标同比约为0.84%，同样处于低增速平台。

回顾本轮修复，2025年下半年至2026年初，剔除食品、能源和黄金后的价格指标明显回升，表明价
格改善并非完全依赖特殊项目；但2026年春季以来，该指标大致趋稳，终端价格修复进入平台期，进一
步上行动力仍显不足。同期，PPI同比滚动均值持续上升，工业品价格走强尚未伴随终端基础消费价格的
同步加速，二者的分化尚无收敛迹象。但基础消费价格保持相对稳定，也暂未显示终端需求压力持续加
深。

（二）涨价动能与正翘尾支撑短期PPI
短期内，PPI同比仍有两方面支撑：一是当期涨价动能尚未消退，10月PPI环比上涨0.7%，显示工业品
价格仍在上行；二是年内已经发生的价格上涨，将通过正翘尾对2027年同比读数形成支撑。
假设2026年11-12月价格维持10月水平，按现有口径测算，2027年1月PPI翘尾因素可拉动同比上涨近5

个百分点。正翘尾对2027年PPI同比的较强支撑可延续至一季度，4月明显减弱，7-8月阶段性回升，9月大
幅消退。这一假设用于衡量截至10月的既有涨价的滞后影响。考虑到当前价格上涨动能仍在，将年末价格
维持在10月水平作为现阶段的基准情景，具有一定合理性。
从结构看，按底稿行业权重近似测算，在2026年末价格维持当前水平的假设下，2027年1月，石油、
煤炭及其他燃料加工业,化学原料及化学制品制造业,计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业,煤炭开采和洗
选业的翘尾贡献分别约为1.37、1.08、0.63和0.62个百分点。四个行业权重合计约24.7%，贡献合计约
3.70个百分点，贡献当期PPI同比翘尾的74.2%。因此，短期PPI同比读数有较强支撑，但价格修复基础尚
待巩固。悲观情形，若能源化工价格回落的同时，中下游价格未能接续修复，并伴随降价范围扩大和订单
走弱，则即使PPI同比处于高位，也需关注价格下行压力重新累积。

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 11 月 11 日

1 群益投顧 
台積電 (2330 TT) 投資 OpenAI 及 Anthropic 延續過去參與 Arm (ARM US)  IPO 的少數
策 略 股 權 模 式 ， 惟 布 局 由 半 導 體 供 給 生 態 系 進 一 步 延 伸 至AI 模 型 與 算 力 需 求 端 。 透
過投資 OpenAI 及 Anthropic 各最高 5 億 美 元 ， 增 加 對 未 來 算 力 需 求 及 運 算 架 構 演 進
的 掌 握 ， 作 為 先 進 製 程 與 封 裝 產 能 規 劃 的 參 考 ，但 不 代 表 取 得 訂 單 或 控 制 權 。現在 台
積 電 的 產 能 和 良 率 優 於 同 業 ，可是 未來 美 國 有 可 能 因 環 境 能 耗 與 安 全 疑 慮 對AI 數據
中 心 及 尖 端 模 型 祭 出 強 硬 監 管 ， 並 取 消 稅 收 補 貼 ， 引 發 市 場 擔 憂 科 技 巨 頭 放 緩AI 資
本 支 出 ， 間 接 影 響 台 積 電 的 業 績 成 長 動 能 ， 因 此 建 議 台 積 電Trading Buy 。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

台 積 電 並 非 首 次 策 略 性 投 資 產 業 鏈 公 司 ， 惟 此 次 首 度 將 布 局 延 伸 至AI 算力
需 求 端 ：  
台積電於 11/10/2026 召 開 董 事 會 ， 分 別 核 准 兩 項 投 資 案 ： 一 是 以 不 超 過 美
金 5 億 元 設 立 投 資 公 司，持 續 進 行 半 導 體 產 業 及 相 關 生 態 系 之 策 略 性 投 資；
二 是 分 別 以 不 超 過 美 金 5 億 元 認 購 Anthropic, PBC 普 通 股 及 OpenAI Group 
PBC 特 別 股，兩 項 直 接 股 權 投 資 合 計 最 高 10 億 美 元。其 中， Anthropic 每股
認購價格將依該公司未來首次公開發行 (IPO) 價格而定。值得注意的是，新
設 投 資 公 司 與 直 接 投 資 兩 家 AI 模 型 業 者 為 兩 項 獨 立 的 董 事 會 決 議 ， 目 前 並
無公開資訊顯示兩者具有直接的資金或架構關聯，整體反映台積電一方面
持 續 布 局 半 導 體 產 業 生 態 系 ， 另 一 方 面 首 度 將 直 接 股 權 投 資 延 伸 至AI 模型
與 算 力 需 求 形 成 端 。 
從歷史脈絡觀察，台積電早在 1990 年 代 即 透 過 InveStar 及 VentureTech 
Alliance(VTA) 等 創 投 架 構 投 資 全 球 無 晶 圓 廠(Fabless) 及 新 興 科 技 公 司 ，以及
入股 IMS Nanofabrication 約 10% 以 布 局 關 鍵 多 重 電 子 束 光 罩 技 術 。 其 中 ，
2023 年 以 不 超 過 1 億 美 元 參 與 Arm IPO 是 此 次 投資 最 具 參 考 價 值 的 案 例 ，
台 積 電 當 時 並 非 為 取 得 經 營 控 制 權 ， 而 是 以 少 數 策 略 股 權 維 持 與 關 鍵IP 生
態 系 的 長 期 連 結 。 此 次 對 OpenAI 及 Anthropic 各 最 高 5 億 美 元 的 投 資 ， 本
質上延續了此種非控制性少數策略股權模式，共同反映台積電並非以大額
資 本 主 導 標 的 ， 而 是 透 過 有 限 資 本 強 化 與 關 鍵 生 態 系 夥 伴 的 策 略 連 結 。 
最 大 戰 略 意 義 ： 策 略 股 權 由 半 導 體 供 給 端 進 一 步 延 伸 至AI 算 力 需 求 端：  
過 去 台 積 電 的 策 略 投 資 主 要 集 中 於 晶 片 設 計 、 半 導 體IP 及 關 鍵 設 備 技 術 ，
核 心 目 的 在 於 擴 大 半 導 體 供 給 生 態 系，協 助 客 戶 持 續 開 發 並 製 造 先 進 晶 片，
投資 Arm 即 屬 於 間 接 支 持 相 關 架 構 設 計 與 製 造 需 求 的 模 式 。 
相 較 之 下 ，OpenAI 及 Anthropic 位於 AI 產 業 鏈 的 需 求 形 成 端 ， 模 型 能 力 提
升 、 訓 練 規 模 擴 大 及 推 論 需 求 增 加 ， 最 終 將 帶 動GPU 、 ASIC 、 HBM 及 資 料
中 心 基 礎 設 施 的 算 力 需 求。因 此，投 資 2 家 模 型 業 者 的 策 略 意 義 並 非 直 接 取
得 晶 圓 訂 單 ， 而 是 增 加 台 積 電 接 觸 及 理 解 未 來AI 算 力 需 求 變 化 的 機 會 ， 包
括訓練 (Training) 與推論 (Inference) 需 求 結 構、運 算 架 構 演 進 及 資 料 中 心 基 礎
設 施 需 求 等 ， 作 為 長 期 先 進 製 程 及 先 進 封 裝 產 能 規 劃 的 參 考 。 
相 較 台 積 電 2026 年 預 估 600 億至 640 億 美 元 的 資 本 支 出 規 模 ， 新 設 投 資 公
司的 5 億 美 元 資 金 上 限 僅 約 0.8% ；而 直 接 投 資 兩 家 模 型 業 者 合 計 最 高 10 億
美 元 ， 相 較 2 者 各 自 超 過 1,000 億 美 元 的 資 金 規 模 及 高 估 值 亦 屬 有 限 。 
若與 OpenAI 及 Anthropic 既 有 主 要 策 略 投 資 者 相 比 ， 台 積 電 各 最 高 5 億美

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 11 月 11 日

2 群益投顧 
元 的 投 資 規 模 明 顯 較 小 。 過 去Microsoft (MSFT US) 、SoftBank (9984 JP) 等業
者對 OpenAI 的 投 資 金 額 達 數 十 億 甚 至 百 億 美 元 級 別 ；Amazon (AMZN US)
及 Google (GOOL US) 對 Anthropic 的 累 計 投 資 亦 達 數 十 億 美 元 。 相 較 之 下 ，
台 積 電 此 次 各 5 億 美 元 的 投 資，顯 然 並 非 以 資 金 主 導，取 得 控 制 權，董 事 會
席次 、主 導 公 司 經 營 為或 追 求 短 期 財 務 回 報 為 目 的，而 是 以 相 對 有 限 的 資 本
建 立 策 略 關 係。惟 實 際 持 股 比 例 仍 須 視 認 購 價 格、股 權 條 件 及 後 續 稀 釋 情 況
而定，目前亦無資訊顯示台積電因此取得參與模型公司技術決策或運算架
構 (Compute Roadmap) 制 定 的 權 利 。 
對 台 積 電 而 言 ， 投 資 AI 模 型 業 者 亦 有 助 於 擴 大 觀 察 未 來 算 力 需 求 的 來 源 ，
而 非 完 全 依 賴 單 一 晶 片 供 應 商 的 產 品 規 劃。無 論 未 來 模 型 業 者 採 用NVIDIA 
GPU 、 AMD GPU 、 Google TPU 、 AWS Trainium 或其他自研 ASIC ， 只 要 相
關晶片及先進封裝採用台積電技術，即可能轉化為台積電的製程與封裝需
求 ， 策 略 價 值 在 於 增 加 對 需 求 端 運 算 架 構 發 展 方 向 的 理 解 。 
仍 須 面 對 客 戶 中 立 性 、 訂 單 轉 換 及 股 權 評 價 等 風 險：  
這 項 投 資 案，仍 有 幾 項 風 險。首 先，台 積 電 必 須 維 持 客 戶 中 立 性。OpenAI 及
Anthropic 分 別 與 Microsoft 、Google 及 Amazon 等 大 型 雲 端 業 者 存 在 密 切 合
作 關 係 ， 並 涉 及 NVIDIA 、 AMD 、 Broadcom 及其他 AI 晶 片 供 應 鏈 。 台 積 電
直接投資特定模型業者，可能使部分客戶關注其資訊隔離及資源配置的中
立 性。此 次 採 取 各 最 高 5 億 美 元 的 少 數 股 權 投 資，且 同 時 投 資 兩 家 主 要 模 型
業 者，有 助 降 低 市 場 對 單 一 選 邊 的 疑 慮，但 台 積 電 後 續 仍 須 維 持 嚴 格 的 商 業
資 訊 保 密 及 客 戶 中 立 原 則 。 
其 次 ， 股 權 投 資 不 等 於 取 得 訂 單 或 產 能 承 諾 。OpenAI 及 Anthropic 並非直
接 採 購 晶 圓 的 客 戶，其 算 力 需 求 仍 須 透 過 CSP 、GPU 及 ASIC 供 應 商 轉 化 為
台 積 電 的 晶 圓 與 先 進 封 裝 需 求。因 此，這 筆 投 資 本 身 並 不 代 表 產 能 長 約、保
證取貨 (Take -or -Pay) 承 諾 或 未 來 訂 單 保 證 ， 實 際 效 益 仍 取 決 於 AI 算 力 需 求
的 持 續 成 長 ， 以 及 相 關 晶 片 供 應 鏈 採 用 台 積 電 技 術 的 程 度 。 
最後， AI 模 型 業 者 目 前 仍 處 於 高 資 本 投 入 及 快 速 競 爭 階 段 。 若 未 來 模 型 服
務 競 爭 加 劇、商 業 化 速 度 低 於 預 期，或 雲 端 業 者 的AI 資 本 支 出 回 報 率 下 降，
導 致 算 力 投 資 放 緩 ， 均 可 能 影 響 OpenAI 及 Anthropic 的 估 值 及 後 續 融 資 能
力 ， 進 而 帶 來 台 積 電 股 權 投 資 的 評 價 波 動 風 險 。 
整 體 而 言，台 積 電 此 次 策 略 並 非 改 變 晶 圓 代 工 本 業，而 是 延 續 過 去 以 少 數 策
略 股 權 連 結 關 鍵 產 業 生 態 系 的 做 法 。 若 說 投 資 Arm 主 要 是 鞏 固 晶 片 如 何 被
設 計 與 製 造 的 供 給 生 態 系 ， 投 資 OpenAI 與 Anthropic 則 是 進 一 步 接 觸 為 何
需 要 更 多 晶 片 與 算 力 的 需 求 形 成 端。對 台 積 電 而 言，以 相 對 有 限 的 資 本 投 入
增加對 AI 產 業 長 期 需 求 變 化 的 理 解 ， 其 策 略 意 義 可 能 高 於 單 純 的 財 務 投 資
報酬。  
11/2026 台 積 電 董 事 會決 議 如 下 ：  
11 /11 /202 6 台 積 電 召 開 董 事 會 ， 會 中 重 要 決 議 如 下 ： 
1) 核准 3Q26 營 業 報 告 書 及 財 務 報 表 ，3Q26 合 併 營 收 約 新 台 幣 1.49 兆 元 ，
稅 後 純 益 約 新 台 幣 7,630 億 元 ， 稅 後 EPS 29.43 元。  
2) 核准配發 3Q26 每 股 現 金 股 利 9.0 元 。 台 積 電 1Q26 和 2Q26 各分派 7 元，

3 群益投顧 
3Q26 發 9 元，依 據 台 積 電 股 利 只 升 不 降，4Q26 至 少 配 發 9 元，也 就 是 2026
年將配發 32 元 以 上 。 
3) 核 准 資 本 預 算 約 美 金 409.27 億 元，內 容 主 要 包 括：1. 建 置 及 升 級 先 進 製 程
產 能 ；2. 建 置 及 升 級 先 進 封 裝 、 成 熟 及/或 特 殊 製 程 產 能 ；3. 廠 房 興 建 及 廠 務
設 施 工 程 ；4. 2027 年 研 發 資 本 預 算 、 經 常 性 資 本 預 算 及 資 本 化 租 賃 資 產 預
算。  
4) 核 准 以 不 超 過 美 金 5 億 元 之 資 金 設 立 一 投 資 公 司，以 進 行 半 導 體 產 業 及 相
關 生 態 系 之 策 略 性 投 資 。 
5) 核 准 於 不 超 過 美 金 5 億 元 之 額 度 內 認 購 Anthropic, PBC 普 通 股 股 份，每 股
認 購 價 格 將 依 該 公 司 首 次 公 開 發 行 之 價 格 而 定 ； 以 及 於 不 超 過 美 金 5 億元
之 額 度 內 認 購 OpenAI Group PBC 特 別 股 股 份 。 
台 積 電 10 /202 6 營收 5,8 68 .9 億元， MoM +14 .65 %， YoY+5 9.70 %：  
台積電公告 10 /2026 營收 5,868 .90 億 元 ， MoM +14 .65 %， YoY+ 59 .70 %，創
歷 史 新 高 。 先 前 台 積 電 法 說 會 預估 4Q2 6 營收 523 ~543 億美元，
QoQ+ 11 .94 %~+ 16 .22 %，中 間 值+14 .08 %。在 1 美元 兌 新 台 幣 31.8 元 基 礎 上，
新 台 幣 營 收 為 1. 66 兆元 ~1. 73 兆元， QoQ+ 13 .43 %~+ 15 .56 % 。 以 此 推 估 ，
11 /2026 和 12 /2026 單 月 平 均 營 收 在 5,381 億元 ~5,699 億 元 ， MoM -2.89 %~ -
8.31 %。  
高 階 製 程 持 續 帶 動 台 積 電 營 收 成 長 ， 台 積 電 預 估 4Q26 毛 利 率 將 介 於 65% -
67% ，以 中 間 值 66% 計 算，較 3Q26 下降約 0.4 個 百 分 點。主 要 是 因 為 N2 技
術 的 快 速 爬 坡，預 期 將 稀 釋 毛 利 率 約 3 個 百 分 點，此 稀 釋 將 被 領 先 技 術 的 強
勁需求與持續的成本改善 ( 含生產力提升 ) 部分抵消。預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 
32.64 元。 2026 年 EPS 111.39 元。  
儘 管 消 費 性( 如 PC 、 智 慧 型 手 機) 與 價 格 敏 感 終 端 市 場 面 臨 零 組 件 價 格 上 漲
與 總 體 經 濟 不 確 定 性 之 挑 戰 ， 台 積 電 對2027 年 對 此 抱 持 審 慎 樂 觀 態 度 ， 但
受惠於領先先進製程之技術差異化與廣泛客戶基礎，及代理人 AI(Agentic 
AI) 推動 AI 助 理 普 及 、 開 源 模 型 降 低 使 用 門 檻 ， 進 一 步 刺 激CPU 與 網 路 晶
片 需 求 ， 預 估 台 積 電 2027 年 稅 後 EPS 147.10 元 。 長 期 而 言 ， 台 積 電 憑 藉 在
先 進 製 程 與 先 進 封 裝 的 高 市 佔 率，實 質 基 本 面 依 然 強 勁。然 而，美 國選 舉 後，
有 可 能 因 環 境 能 耗 與 安 全 疑 慮 對 AI 數 據 中 心 及 尖 端 模 型 祭 出 強 硬 監 管 ， 並
取 消 稅 收 補 貼， 引 發 市 場 擔 憂 科 技 巨 頭 放 緩 AI 資 本 支 出 ， 間 接 影 響 台 積 電
的 業 績 成 長 動 能 ， 因 此 建 議 台 積 電 Trading Buy 。

4 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部對義隆電的投資建議維持中立 ， 係因(1)受到產業影響 ，3Q26
財報大幅不如預期 ，(2)4Q26 淡季更淡 ， 預估下修幅度不小 ，(3)27
年產業狀況仍受到 CPU、記憶體等元件影響，目前較難樂觀看待，
(4)下修 26、27 年獲利預估。綜合以上，下修義隆電 26 年 EPS 預
估為 6.26 元(原估 9.37 元，-22%YOY)，及預估 27 年 EPS 為 6.58
元(原估 10.14 元，+5.1%YOY)，因為 NB 市場除了先前的記憶體影
響外 ， 目前更受到CPU 缺貨的影響 ， 短線上義隆電的營運動能受到
連帶影響大 ， 靜待無人機相關IC 營收在 2H27 放大 ， 故投資建議維
持中立，目標價 132 元。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26 營收大幅滑落，獲利不如預期： 
義隆電 3Q26 營收僅為 22.2 億元(原估 30.85 億元)，衰退
30.7%QOQ，衰退33%YOY，營收大規模衰退，除了記憶體漲
價外，因為Agent AI 興起，使得CPU 缺貨嚴重，另外因為
成本反映至終端產品，故使得需求更是急凍。毛利率僅
44.7%，相對於2Q26 下滑 2.7 個百分點，主要就是各類成
本漲價，包含晶圓代工、封裝及 TOUCH PAD 內的 PCB 及被
動元件等，另外產品組合也轉差所致。另外業外又有高達
1 億元的金融評價損失，故使得稅後淨利僅為 1.68 億元
(原估 6.23 億元)，衰退 80.9%QOQ，衰退 79.1%YOY，EPS 
0.55 元(原估 EPS 2.05 元)。3Q26 產品組合為：AI 14%，
觸控 73%， 非觸控13%， 其中TOUCH PAD 43%，TOUCH SCREEN 
14%，指紋 16%，POINT STICK 19%，MCU+SENSOR 4%。

2. 3Q26 未給展望，但受到 CPU 缺貨影響嚴重： 
義隆電因為最近四季合併（個別）財務報表營業外收支淨
額合計數占稅前損益合計數之絕對值若大於 10%，在法說
會中不能提及預測性資訊，但在法說會有提到 4Q26 本來
就是季節性淡季，另外客戶在上半年非常積極拉貨，下半
年開始調整庫存，故 4Q26 推估各產線仍有下滑，僅有 AI
相關產品會成長。故研究部下修 4Q26 營收預估為 16.5 億
元(原估 31.7 億元)，衰退25.7%QOQ，衰退42.3%YOY，稅
後淨利同步下修預估為 1.51 億元(原估 6.38 億元)，衰退
10.1%QOQ， 衰退79.5%YOY，EPS 0.5 元(原估 EPS 2.1 元)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. 對 27 年 NB 產業看法仍未定，但義隆仍有新商機： 
義隆電在法說會表示，NB 產業有各研調機構都有看法，目
前普遍較為保守，但未來仍端視 27 年記憶體、各元件及
CPU 的狀況，非單一供應商決定市況，故認為在此階段仍
不易下結論。但義隆電在 NB 觸控相關 IC 方面，因為美系
競爭對手被併購後 ， 義隆電有SHARE GAIN 部分市場 ， 另外
也提供更廣泛的產品選項供客戶選擇，故公司認為本身的
NB 相關 IC 出貨量 27 年將優於市場表現。

4. 無人機貢獻性確實提升，大貢獻性將落在 2H27： 
1H26 無人機營收占比約低個位數，3Q26 已經達到 14%，
4Q26 有望在持續攀升。目前主要配合的客戶為國內廠商，
該國內廠商前天六項標案全數得標，而義隆電現在主要出
貨的產品為 CAMERA 模組，目前為兩個鏡頭一個模組 +AI 
BOX，單價為數十美元，目前送樣 4 鏡頭+避障+AI BOX 的
解決方案，單價高達 800 美元，有望在 1H27 逐步開始量
產，下半年完整貢獻。推估 AI 產品營收在 27 年成長約
30%YOY 以上。

5. 大幅下修26年預估，27年預估也較為保守：  
3Q26受到客戶已經提前拉貨影響，加上相關零組件及封裝
成本提升，使得3Q26營收及獲利大幅不如預期，加上4Q26
又有下修，故研究部下修26年義隆電營收預估為103.02億
元(原估126.8億元)，EPS下修預估至6.26元(原估EPS 9.37
元)。 展望27年 ， 目前研究部以27年NB整體出貨量高雙位數
衰退，但義隆電整體為低雙位數衰退，雖然義隆電無人機
相關產品推進至整合型產品，單價大幅提升下，仍下修27
年營收預估為112.3億元(原估140億元)， 成長9%YOY， 稅後
淨利下修預估為20億元(原估30.8億元)，成長5.1%YOY，
EPS 6.58元(原估10.14元)。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上

降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下

Idea
Wiwynn Corp  | Asia Pacific
3Q beat on higher GM/OpM
Recent concerns revolve around potential data center delays,
though the supply chain has not seen any changes. However, we
lower our mid-term growth  rate to bake in some conservatism.
Our PT now implies a lower CY27 P/E at  12x. RR looks attractive
at 7.7x CY27 P/E vs. the 3Y  avg. of ~10x.
4Q revenue should continue to grow double-digits q/q: Wiwynn announced
October revenue at ~NT$165B (-4% m/m, +81% y/y). If we assume the October

monthly run-rate can be sustained, 4Q should grow at least 13-14% q/q. However,
we think ASIC server racks will continue to ramp-up through-out year-end, so we
forecast 4Q revenue to ~20% q/q to reach ~NT$520B (+78% y/y). Higher cost
structure and higher mix of ASIC server racks should drive margins to decline q/q to
~8.5% (-40bps q/q), resulting in net profit of ~NT$27.7B or EPS of NT$46.70.
ASIC/GPU projects remain on track: Our checks indicate that  Trainium3 server rack

production is ramping up well at Wiwynn, and with T3, Wiwynn is a more
comprehensive partner, also engaging in UBB and switch trays. It will also engage in
AMD's (covered by Joe Moore) MI455 Helios rack assembly for customers such as
Meta (covered by Brian Nowak) and Oracle (covered by Sanjit Singh). We forecast
Wiwynn to build ~8K Helios racks for CY27.
3Q results beat on better margins: Revenue was NT$434.6B (+63% y/y) with GM
of 8.9% (-40bps q/q), which is 90bps/30bps above MSe/Street. OP reached ~NT

$32.5B (+66% y/y) for OpM of 7.5% (+20bps q/q), which is 100bps/50bps above
MSe/Street, driven by operating leverage. Non-op losses were slightly higher than
MSe/Street mainly due to higher interest expense, resulting in net profit of ~NT
$23.4B (+52% y/y) for EPS of NT$42.09, 44%/10% above MSe/Street.
Reiterate OW with price target unchanged at NT$2,515 (12x/11x CY27/28e P/E) –
our higher EPS estimates are offset by a lower mid-term growth rate. Risk-reward is

highly attractive at 7.7x CY27e or 6.7x CY28e P/E vs. the three-year range of 6-16x,
with a strong pipeline of AI and non-AI server projects.

Price target discussion and valuation methodology
We keep our price target unchanged at NT$2,515: This implies 12x CY27e P/E or 11x CY28
P/E, vs. its thee-year average forward P/E of 10.3x, which we think is fair, in view of
Wiwynn's AI server project wins and share gains for general-purpose servers in the next 1-
2 years.
We continue to use a residual income model to derive our price target. Key assumptions
include a cost of equity of 7.0% (beta of 1.0, equity premium of 6% and risk-free rate of

1%), a medium-term growth rate of 4% (lowered from 6%), and a terminal growth rate of
3%.
Our bull and bear case scenario values are also unchanged at NT$3,210 and NT$1,470,
respectively.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Update
Lite-On Technology  | Asia Pacific
October  Sales +6% MoM/+32%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Lite-On's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• October sales came in at NT$20,071mn (+6% MoM/+32% YoY), driven by the
server PSU/BBU and opto semiconductors (couplers and indicators for
industrial automation, new energy and AI applications).
• QTD revenue reached 33% of both MSe at NT$60,395mn (+5% QoQ/+36%

YoY), and consensus at NT$60,353mn (+5% QoQ/+36% YoY).
• By segment, Cloud & AIoT/Optoelectronics/IT&CE accounted for
66%/13%/21% of the monthly sales, respectively, implying growth of +19%/-
1%/-17% MoM and  +71%/+7%/-16% YoY.
Our view:
• We think its 4Q26 revenue is tracking slightly ahead of market expectations.
• We believe Lite-On will grow along with the AI capex cycle driven by its
power-related offerings, though execution and progress with new customers

remain key to watch.
• Stay EW.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lite-On Technology (2301.TW)
Base case: P/E multiple of 15x based on average 2027-28 EPS estimates. We believe our target
multiple is justified by the 50% earnings CAGR we estimate for 2025-28, implying a PEG of
0.3, vs. the stock's 16x P/E and 2% earnings CAGR during 2022-25, which implied a PEG of 7.2.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger optical business growth
n Stronger data center/server demand

n PC/NB demand growth
n Downtrend in raw material prices
Risks to Downside
n Macro demand weakness
n Weaker data center/server demand
n PC/NB demand slowdown
n Uptrend in raw material prices

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following on our results Memo, we lower our forecasts and 
TP for M31, as we factor in its still suboptimal scale and delayed 
employment bonus in 3Q26 that weigh on its trajectory of a bottom-line 
turnaround. That said, we remain comfortable with our Buy (1) call on the 
stock as we expect M31’s turnaround, kicking off from 3Q26 despite at a 
small scale, to sustain into 2027 with meaningful incremental builds, 
supported by its conversion from license to royalty revenue paying off, and 
broadened footprint into advanced technologies for price accretion and into 
diversified foundry, IDM, and fabless customers for volume accretion.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes. We lower our 2026-27E EPS for 
M31 by 14-43% to embed its still suboptimal scale and opex overhang in 
our model. Despite the cuts, its bottom-line turnaround met our expectation, 
and we expect the turnaround to sustain with meaningful incremental builds 
sequentially through 2027 to improve market confidence. We forecast its 
4Q26 top line to rebound by 23% QoQ (+12% YoY) for EPS to reach 
TWD3.31 (TWD0.35 in 3Q26), setting a good start for M31’s 2027 outlook 
and warranting a strong 213% EPS CAGR over 2026-28E.

Turnaround confirmed. While prolonged license project recognitions 
delayed M31’s turnaround, depicted in our previous Flash, we believe its 
bumpy operations over the past 2 years is finally coming to an end, 
evidenced by management’s comments of QoQ revenue growth for 4Q26 
with EPS expanding to cover accumulated losses YTD. M31 has actively 
invested over the past years enriching its IP portfolio, accumulating more 
than 2,000 license projects since 2012 (chart on p.2), and setting a good 
foundation for converting them to royalty revenue. It aims for the royalty 
revenue to outgrow license revenue in the next few quarters, after years of 
“cooking”. Meanwhile, two structural growth drivers remain intact for ASP 
and volume accretion: 1) for ASP, a growing number of high-value projects 
from advanced process nodes to enhance M31’s IP portfolio for ASP 
accretion, and 2) for volume, an increased diversification of customer base 
globally to broaden its addressable markets for volume accretion.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we lower our 12M TP 
for M31 to TWD550 (previous: TWD645), still based on our 4-quarter 
forward PER of 45x. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating with key downside risks 
of silicon inflation and geopolitical overhang leading to global inflation and 
AI capex cut to delay M31’s royalty ramps.

How we differ: Our 2027E EPS is 52% above the consensus, likely due to 
our more positive view on royalty revenue ramp.

10 November 2026

Structural turnaround round the corner (III)

➢ Lowering our forecasts to reflect still suboptimal scale… 
➢ … but expecting enriched license projects to bear fruit soon 
➢ Reiterating our Buy (1) rating with a lower 12M TP of TWD550

Company profile 
Founded in 2011, M31 is an independent semiconductor IP provider in Taiwan, offering both foundation 
IP for specialty technology nodes and functional IP such as high-speed interface and analogue IP.

### Page 5

5

M31 Technology (6643 TT): 10 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 11 Mar 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
M31's board of directors (BoD) has 7 members, including 3 independent directors, who
represent 43% of total directors, higher than the regulatory requirement of at least 20%. Its
Chairman and President are not the same person, but its President is one of the directors. M31
has the Audit Committee, Compensation Committee and Risk Control Committee under the
BoD.
Capital management 1
Although M31 is a young company (founded in 2011 and listed in 2019), its dividend payout ratio
has been over the 75% level for the past 5 years. M31 is a semiconductor IP provider without
onsite production activities, which would require high capex to do so. As such, we view its high
dividend payout ratio to be appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1 We do not see any meaningful impact from related-party transactions.
S Supply chain management 2
M31's suppliers are required to comply with the Supplier Management Standards, which was set
by M31 in 2013. In addition, M31 has periodical evaluations for its suppliers and onsite audits to
ensure the quality and implementation of Corporate Social Responsibility (CSR), as well as
Environmental Protection and Safety and Integrity Policies.
E Water & wastewater management 1 M31 is a semiconductor IP provider without physical products, and thus no production activity to affect the environment.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
The company is a semiconductor IP provider without physical products, and thus no production
activity to affect the environment. It still adopts some measures to reduce waste from its
operations, such as recycling and using electronic files to replace paper documents as much as
possible to reduce the use of paper.
E GHG emissions 1 M31 is a semiconductor IP provider without physical products, and thus no production activity to affect the environment.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

AWSC’s 3Q26 core business OP was NT$209mn, which was lower 
than both GSe/BBG consensus at NT$226mn/NT$250mn, mainly 
due to the ongoing increase in raw material cost, leading to a 24.3% 
GM (lower than GSe/BBG consensus GM at 25.6%/27.8%), despite 
UTR in 3Q26 remaining stable at 65-70%. Moreover, AWSC reported 
NI of NT$191mn, which was 3% higher than GSe but 17% lower than 
the BBG consensus due to the GM contraction. 
Going into 4Q26, we believe the company’s revenue will increase by 
1% as key business lines (including WiFi, cellular and VCSEL) should 
remain stable in 4Q26 as we expect the company’s utilization rate to 
remain stable at 65-70%. Moreover, we expect its GM to rise to 
27.2% (up 2.9ppt QoQ) due to the material cost increase slowing 
down. 
We continue to view rising material costs as the key risk to AWSC’s 
profitability, as the company is now securing raw materials at higher 
cost as low-cost inventory has already been depleted in 1H26 amid 
the strong demand pull in. The company continues to negotiate 
price increases with clients and expects the adjustments to be fully 
reflected starting in 1Q27. However, we remain cautious on the 
low-end cellular segment, where customer demand tends to be 
more price-sensitive. 
For the AI-related business, the company continues to ship VCSEL 
products for 400G/800G optical transceiver this year, with revenue 
contribution to be <2% in 2026E per our estimates. Meanwhile, the 
company continues to make progress on the new PD (Photodiode) 
product and is currently undergoing verification with several 
customers. The company expects PD products to start seeing 
revenue contribution starting from 2H27 or 2027-end given its long 
verification process, typically 6 months+, thus we expect revenue 
contribution to be limited at <2%/~5% in 2027E/2028E. 
Investment view

### Page 3

We maintain our conservative view on the cellular/WiFi PA industry (see here) as we 
continue to see weaker smartphone shipment (-10%/+2%/+2% in 2026/27/28, see 
here) as the smartphone market continues to be impacted by the elevated memory price 
(see here). 
For AWSC, we believe the company could further gain market share among China PA 
design houses as Chinese clients increasingly shift toward higher-end PA products, 
supported by the company’s superior cost control capability and higher production yield 
relative to peers. In addition, AWSC has continued to expand its market share with a 
Korean smartphone brand, which should provide further growth momentum to its 
cellular business in coming quarters/years. Overall, we expect AWSC to continue gaining 
share from its key competitors due to (1) its competitive pricing strategy, underpinned 
by a more favorable cost structure comparing to its key competitor, Win Semi 
(AWSC/Win Semi’s fixed cost accounts for 30%/40% of the total, while Win Semi’s COGS 
per wafer is ~130% higher than AWSC’s) and (2) its strategic focus on China- and 
US-based PA design houses. 
We continue to see a recovery in 2026 CAPEX plans among Taiwan PA foundry players. 
However, we do not view this as a signal of a meaningfully stronger demand outlook as 
the capital spending is more related to new AI businesses. AWSC revised up its 2026 
CAPEX guidance to NT$600-650mn (from NT$500-600mn) and continues to expect 
CAPEX to increase in 2027/28. Also, management continues to expect UTR to reach at 
most ~70% in 2026, implying no need for capacity expansion in the near term while the 
additional capex will be spent on new optical related business. 
Overall, we maintain our Neutral rating on AWSC with a new 12-m TP of NT$136 (from 
NT$147) as we roll over our valuation period by one quarter. Post results, we revise 
down our 2027E/28E earnings by 10%/10% to reflect lower revenue and the negative 
impact from rising material cost (GM contraction), revise up 2026E earnings by 2%, and 
introduce our 2029E estimates.

### Page 5

Earnings revisions  
We revise up our 2026E revenue by 1% to factor in the 3Q26 actual results and we revise 
down 2027E/28E revenue by 9%/10% assuming a more conservative outlook for the 
cellular business; we revise down 2026E/27E/28E GM estimates by 0.4/0.7/0.8ppt to 
reflect the negative impact from rising material cost. Overall, we revise down 2027E/28E 
earnings by 10%/10% and revise up 2026E earnings by 2%. 
In this report, we introduce our 2029E estimates.

Valuation 
We maintain our Neutral rating and revise down our TP from NT$147 to NT$136, based 
on a lower 25x target P/E (from 27x previously, 0.5 STDV below the company’s past 
10-year forward P/E multiple), factoring in our earnings revisions and, consistent with 
our group coverage practices, rolling over our valuation period from 4Q26-3Q27E to 
2027E.

### Page 6

Investment Thesis and TP & risks

AWSC is one of the largest GaAS foundries globally, with 10-15% market share in the 
GaAS foundry industry in 2025. We expect the company to enjoy solid share gain 
opportunity in the long term, considering its much lower than peers’ production cost 
and better production efficiency. However, given the high inventory level in the supply 
chain, and the slower-than-expected Android smartphone PA demand in coming 
quarters, we expect relatively slow profit growth in the near term. 
We are Neutral rated given the company’s relatively slow profit growth in coming 
quarters. We think the stock is priced fairly, trading at its historical upcycle average P/E 
multiple, despite the long-term market share growth opportunity. We forecast AWSC’s 
net income to grow at a 34% 2026E-28E CAGR. The key up/downside risks are (1) 
easing/rising competition in the China PA foundry market, (2) 
faster/slower-than-expected progress on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade trends, and (3) 
faster/slower-than expected progress with new businesses, including LEO satellite, 
drones and datacom.  
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$136 is based on a target 25x 
2027E P/E, which is 0.5 STDV below the company’s past 10-year forward P/E multiple 
and the inventory build-up period average P/E. We consider this multiple appropriate 
given the soft demand outlook and our positive view on the company’s longer-term 
share gain opportunity. 
The key up/downside risks are 1) easing/rising competition in the China PA foundry 
market, (2) faster/slower-than-expected progress on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade trends, and (3) 
faster/slower-than expected progress with new businesses, including LEO satellite, 
drones and datacom.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Asia Vital Components

Solid 3Q26 results; positive tone to 4Q26/ 
2027; reiterate Buy; trim PO to NT$3,650

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 3,650 TWD | Price: 2,790 TWD

3Q26: record-high GM/OPM for five straight quarters 
Asia Vital Components (AVC)’s 3Q26 GM/OPM of 33.9%/28.5% hit a record high for the 
fifth straight quarter, exceeding BofAe of 33.0%/27.5% and consensus of 33.3%/27.9%. 
OP (+25% QoQ, +108% YoY) was 7%/4% above BofAe/consensus, with higher sales 
(+20% QoQ, +52% YoY; 3%/2% above BofAe/consensus). We attribute the results beat 
to solid product mix (server and networking comprised 66% of 3Q26 sales, same as 1Q-
2Q26) and improving production efficiency. Despite a non-operating loss (mainly FX 
loss), NP was 10%/3% above consensus (Exhibit 3).  
Positive tone to 4Q26/2027; ongoing capacity expansion 
We expect 4Q26 sales to rise 19% QoQ and 2027 sales to grow 40% YoY, with mgmt.’s 
still positive tone to 4Q26/2027, mainly driven by AI liquid cooling projects. AVC 
indicated full support to clients’ platform upgrade and shipment pipeline. It should be 
able to pass through the higher raw material cost. In 2027, mgmt. targets ASIC sales mix 
to surpass GPU. AVC also sees solid sales momentum from chassis/rack and CDU, acting 
as the solution provider. Mgmt. maintained 2026 capex guidance at NT$15bn and guided 
NT$15-20bn for 2027 (BofAe unchanged at NT$17bn), mainly for capacity expansion in 
Vietnam. Mgmt. now targets another 50% cold plate capacity expansion in 2027. 
Trim PO to NT$3,650 for a lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We lift 2026-28E EPS by 3-10%, mainly to reflect the 3Q26 results beat and higher 
margins, driven by ongoing server/networking project ramp-up. Yet, we cut our PO to 
NT$3,650 (23x 2027E EPS), with a lower P/E multiple (2SD above average since 2020; 
Exhibit 5) from NT$3,888 (was 27x; 3SD above) on AI supply chain’s potentially higher 
shipment/pipeline uncertainty due to component shortage. Nevertheless, we reiterate 
Buy on AVC as we believe it should remain one of the key beneficiaries from rising 
liquid-cooling adoption and ongoing GPU/ASIC platform/spec upgrade. We also highlight 
solid sales/margin growth under broad product base and diversified project/client base.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
AVC is the leading system-level thermal/cooling component 
maker globally. Key end markets include server, PC, 
smartphone. Key end clients include global CSPs, PC brands, 
smartphone brands. AVC was founded in 1991 and went 
publicly listed on Taiwan Stock Exchange on 27 Sep 2002. It 
is headquartered in Taiwan. Its main production sites are 
located in China (Shenzhen, Wuhan, Shanghai, Dongguan, 
Chengdu), with ongoing capacity expansion in Vietnam.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to AVC given its leading position in 
thermal/cooling component and total solution strategy to 
ride on strong AI market demand, double-digit sales/EPS 
CAGR in 2026-28 with rising liquid cooling sales mix and 
ASP/margin increase.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
Asia Vital Components (AVMPF) 
Our PO of NT$3,650 is based on 23x 2027E EPS. We use 23x P/E multiple (2SD above 
P/E average since 2020) in view of AVC's double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E. We 
believe AVC's leading position in thermal/cooling component market and strong AI 
market demand will drive rising liquid cooling sales mix and ASP/margin increase.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) stronger demand in AI server, server, PC, smartphone, (2) 
better operating leverage with larger revenue scale, (3) less intensive competition, (4) 
faster product certification, (5) faster capacity expansion, (6) favorable FX movement 
(USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to help on GM and/or non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/component 
shortage, (2) weaker server/PC/smartphone market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (3) GM pressure due to higher material/labor/utility cost, (4) more 
intensive competition, (5) delayed product certification, (6) slower capacity expansion, 
(7) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-
operating items.

iQmethod SMis the set of BofA Global Research standard measures that serve to maintain global consistency under three broad headings: Business Performance, Quality of Earnings, and validations. The key features of 
iQmethod are: A consistently structured, detailed, and transparent methodology. Guidelines to maximize the effectiveness of the comparative valuation process, and to identify some common pitfalls. 
iQdatabase ®  is our real-time global research database that is sourced directly from our equity analysts’ earnings models and includes forecasted as well as historical data for income statements, balance sheets, and cash

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 -  Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things , 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst ’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

3Q26 OP 2.7조, 당사 추정치 35% 상회 
서프라이즈의 근거가 판매보증비 감소에서 발생. 3Q26 기말환율의 낙폭 (-12% qoq)으로 
인한 역설적 비용절감의 규모가 전년동기 대비 1.2조 원을 넘어서며 환율의 조사모삼 효과
로 만들어진 컨센서스 상회 실적. 
긍정적인 부분: 컨틴전시, 신차 효과, 인센티브, 생산 정상화 
지속적인 컨틴전시 체제를 유지하며 비용절감 실천 (=지속된 요소). 4분기 추가적으로 나아
질 요인은 1) 내수 신차효과로 판가 상승 및 유기적 수익성 개선, 2) 공장 가동률 정상화를 
통한 고정비 부담 절감, 3) 해외시장 신차효과를 통한 딜러 인센티브 절감. 
경계할 부분: 재료비, 환율, BEV 판매비중 확대 
재료비 측면에서 1) 2Q26 시작된 미국-이란 전쟁 관련 원재료비 상승은 4Q26F에도 하락
세를 보이지 못할 것이며, 2) 구리, 철강 등의 금속 원자재의 가격 상승 또한 4Q26F 지속될 
것. 또한 3Q26과 반대로 qoq 기말환율 대비 낙폭 확대할 평균환율 (mtd 1,338원) 효과로 
환 관련 감익 본격화, IONIQ 3의 출시는 인센티브 절감인 동시에 믹스 관점에서 부정적. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 440,000원으로 소폭 상향 
목표주가 상향 근거는 컨틴전시 체제 유지를 통한 FY27F 자동차사업 부문 이익 예상치 상
향. 부정적 외생변수 (환율, 재료비)가 지속된다는 전제 하에 FY27F OPM 하락을 예상하나, 
(FY26F 5.6%, FY27F 5.2%) 향후 신차효과와 생산 정상화에 따른 자동차 사업부문 수익성 
반등의 여력 존재. 주가 상승 트리거는 1) CES 전후 BD 관련 기대감, 2) 신차 출시 이후 견
조한 판매량, 3) 환율, 원재료비 등 외생변수의 개선.

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
아직 수요의 끝은 보이지 않는다 
AI 투자에 대한 우려의 목소리도 있지만 정작 부품을 공급하고 투자하는 쪽의 목소
리는 시간이 갈수록 더 커지고 있다. 공급 부족의 시점 매 분기 뒤로 밀려나고 있기 
때문이다. 28년까지 공급부족이라고 했던 메모리업체들은 이구동성으로 30년까지 공
급부족이 지속된다고 언급하고 있다. 이러한 변화는 실적에 고스란히 반영되고 있
다. 26년 3분기 메모리 영업이익만 108.5조원으로 추정한다. 4 분기에는 140조원에 
이를 것으로 전망한다. 
HBM4와 QLC는 차별화 포인트 
삼성전자는 HBM에서 입지가 좋았던 적이 없었다. 점유율, 수익성, 가동률, 제품 성
능, 고객 등 모든 항목에서 경쟁사 대비 열위에 있었다. HBM4는 모든 항목에서 입
지를 개선시킬 변수가 될 것으로 예상한다. 26년 3분기 물량은 경쟁사 수준일 것으
로 보이고 연간 물량도 유사한 수준일 전망이다. 27년에는 가격 인상과 가동률 상승
으로 수익성도 크게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. QLC는 TLC와 함께 AI 모델 진화의 
수혜를 받을 제품으로 빠르게 부상하고 있다. 메타의 Muse 영향이다. 접속자당 가
상 메모리를 NAND에 100GB를 할당하고 있기 때문이다.  
부족한 주주환원으로 투자 우선 순위에서 밀릴 듯 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 
변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, HBM 
시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 
판단하기 때문이다. 목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황
을 고려하면 적정 주가는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치
에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 
제한적일 것으로 판단하기 때문이다.

삼성전자 (005930)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기 매출액은 195.3조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.9% 증가한 195.3조원이다. VD/
가전을 제외한 주요 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 증가했다. DS, MX 모두 물량보다는 
가격 효과가 더 크게 작용한 것으로 분석된다. 사업부별로는  
1) DS 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 15.4% 증가한 147.1조원이다. 메모리
는 26년 2분기 대비 16.4% 증가했다. 26년 3분기 DRAM B/G(Bit Growth)
는 +2.0%, ASP는 +20.0%이다. NAND B/G는 +5.0%, ASP는 +18.0%이다. 
HBM 물량 증가는 HBM4가 주도했고, 그 이하 제품 비중은 하락한 것으로 
추정한다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 7.1% 증가한 8조원이다. 소형
은 해외 고객 및 국내 고객의 신제품 출시 영향으로 ASP 상승, 물량 증가 
효과가 동반되었고, 대형은 게이밍 모니터 비중을 확대했다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.4% 증가한 37.7조원이
다. MX는 15.5% 증가했다. 물량은 소폭 감소했고, ASP 상승 영향을 받았다. 
네트워크 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 60% 감소했다.  
4) VD/가전 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 4.1% 감소한 13.9조원이다. VD 
매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 6.5% 감소했다. 가전은 26년 2분기 대비 1.3% 
감소했다.

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │3 
26년 3분기 영업이익은 107.4조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 20% 증가한 107.4조원이다. DS,  
디스플레이와 MX는 2분기 대비 증가했고, VD/가전은 2분기 대비 감소했다. 사업부별
로는 
1) DS 사업부는 26년 2분기 대비 19.4% 증가한 106.5조원이다. 2분기 대비 영
업이익률은 소폭 상승했다. 메모리 영업이익은 2분기 대비 19.0% 증가했고, 
비메모리는 영업적자를 유지했다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 54.5% 증가한 1.1조원이다. 
중소형 신제품 효과로 수익성이 개선된 영향이다. 대형도 믹스 개선으로 수
익성이 개선되었다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자폭이 감소했다. 물량
은 줄었지만 High End 비중 상승으로 ASP는 개선되었고, 원가 비중이 낮아
진 영향이다. 
4) VD/가전 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자 규모가 커졌다.

그림 2. 삼성전자 사업부별 분기별 영업이익 추이 및 전망

### Page 6

삼성전자 (005930)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기 매출액은 226.5조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 16.0% 증가한 226.5조원으로 예상
한다. DS가 증가하고 나머지는 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS는 메모리가 성장을 주도할 것으로 기대한다. 가격과 물량 모두 3분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 전망한다. Context Server에 대한 수요가 증가하고, Personal Agent의 가상메
모리 설정으로 인해 NAND 수요가 증가하면서 QLC에 대한 수요 증가 속도가 빨라질 
전망이다. 이로 인해 NAND Bit Growth는 경쟁사와 차별화된 수준일 것으로 예상한
다.  
HBM 물량도 꾸준히 증가할 것으로 기대한다. 3 분기에도 HBM4를 중심으로 물량이 
크게 증가했다. HBM4는 SK하이닉스와 비슷한 물량일 것으로 예상한다. 아직 전체 
물량은 SK하이닉스가 더 많은 상황이고, HBM 내 HBM4 비중은 업계 최고 수준인 것
으로 판단한다. 26년까지는 HBM이 수익성에는 마이너스 요인이지만, 27년에는 
DRAM 수준의 영업이익이 가능할 것으로 기대한다.  
MX는 비수기 저가 라인 물량 조정으로 전체 물량은 감소하지만, 제품믹스는 개선되
어 판가는 3분기 대비 상승할 것으로 예상한다.

26년 4분기 영업이익은 138.2조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 28.7% 증가한 138.2조원으로 예
상한다. DS 사업부를 제외하고 모두 3분기 대비 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS 사업부 영업이익은 3분기 대비 30% 증가한 138.5조원으로 전망한다. DRAM, 
NAND 가격이 10% 초중반 수준으로 상승하고, 물량은 10% 중반 이상 증가하면서 3
분기 대비 영업이익률 개선을 기대하기 때문이다. HBM 물량 증가가 이익 개선에 미
치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상한다.  
디스플레이, MX는 물량 부진으로 3분기 대비 영업이익은 부진할 것으로 전망한다.

DS가 성장 주도 
DS만 보자

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
Check Point 
1. 모자란 듯한 주주환원 
26년 3분기 실적 발표에서 밝힌 주주환원 정책은 지난 8월의 내용과 다르지 않다. 3
분기 배당 30조원(주당 4,601원)을 확정한 정도이다. 잔여 배당 재원이 55~75조원으
로 구체화했고, 현금 배당과 자사주 매입을 병행하겠다고 밝혔다.  
자사주 매입 시 우선주 매입도 고려 대상이라고 언급한 것은 고무적이지만 27년부터 
새롭게 시작되는 주주환원 정책을 결정하기 위한 이사회 개최는 27년 1월에 열릴 것
으로 예고하였기에 연내 추가적인 주주환원 발표는 없을 것으로 판단한다.  
2. HBM4로 HBM 시장에서 입지 강화 
HBM4를 통해서 자신감을 회복하고 있는 것으로 판단한다. 양산 시점은 경쟁사 대비 
빨랐고, 3분기 물량은 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준인 것으로 추정한다. HBM3e가 부진했기 
때문에 전체 생산량에서 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 가장 높은 수준일 것으로 추정한다.  
26년 전체 물량도 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준이 될 것으로 전망한다. 삼성전자는 HBM 시
장 점유율을 DRAM 수준으로 맞추겠다는 목표를 3분기에 달성한 것으로 밝혔다.  
물량이 증가하면서 가동률도 빠르게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. 아직은 DRAM과 영업
이익률에서 큰 차이가 있지만 27년에는 인상된 가격, 높아진 수율, 가동률 개선으로 
DRAM과 영업이익률 차이를 좁힐 수 있을 것으로 기대한다.  
3. QLC NAND 시장 확대 
AI 컴퓨팅 확산은 NAND에게도 성장 기회이다. AI 모델들의 Context Window가 길어
져 KV Cache 메모리에 대한 수요가 커지면서 High Endurance TLC 수요가 급증하고 
있다. DRAM을 대체할 수준의 NAND가 필요하게 되었기 때문이다.  
이 뿐만 아니라 고용량 NAND에 대한 수요도 Personal Agent의 출현으로 급속히 증
가할 것으로 기대한다. 여기에는 QLC NAND가 주로 사용된다. 메타의 Muse는 접속
자 1인에 대해서 DRAM 8GB, NAND 100GB의 가상 메모리를 설정하고 운영한다.  
Agent AI의 확산은 스토리지에 대한 수요도 급격히 확대할 것으로 기대하고, 삼성전
자에게도 기회가 될 것으로 기대한다.  
26년 4분기 NAND Bit Growth는 삼성전자가 가장 높을 것으로 예상한다.

삼성전자 (005930)

8 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 350,000원으로 하향 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 
1) 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 
2) 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, 
3) HBM 시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 
4) 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 판단하기 때문이다. 
목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황을 고려하면 적정 주가
는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사
와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 제한적일 것으로 판단하기 
때문이다.

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick_cd366e43.pdf

### Page 1

(012450) 
한화에어로스페이스 
2026.10.30

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 1,660,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 760,000원(10/29)

 3Q26 연결 매출액은 6조 943억원(-6.0%yoy), 영업이익은 4,520억원(-
46.2%yoy, OPM 7.4%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센 1조 1,614억원을 하회.  
 지상방산의 손익이 크게 악화. 영업이익은 217억원(OPM 1.7%) 기록. 주요 
납품이 4분기에 집중된 가운데 일회성 비용 약 500억원(대전사업장 복구·
정상화 약 300억원, 국내 사업 충당금 약 160억원)과 환율 영향이 겹침.   
 다만 내용을 살펴보면 수출 사업의 수익성 훼손은 크지 않았다는 판단임.  
 일회성 제외 영업이익은 약 717억원(OPM 5.8%)이며, 일회성 비용은 대전
사업장이 국내 물량을 전담한다는 점에서 전액 국내 귀속으로 추정. 내수 
OPM을 일회성 제외 -5% 내외로 가정하면, 내수 영업이익은 약 -826억원
(일회성 포함 OPM -12.7%), 수출은 1,043억원(OPM 17.6%)으로 추정됨. 
 1Q26 대비로는 국내 마진 하락이, 2Q26 대비로는 수출 물량 감소가 가장 
크게 작용(도표 참고). 국내는 저마진 사업 매출이 늘고 고마진 반복 양산 물
량이 줄며 일시적으로 믹스가 악화. 수출은 폴란드 등 주요 인도가 정체되어 
매출이 감소한 가운데 저수익 사업 중심으로 믹스가 악화된 영향. 
 4Q26부터 회복 구간 진입 전망. 폴란드 K9 2차 실행계약 물량의 현지 인
도가 본격화되며, 회사는 2026년 폴란드 K9 30문 이상, 천무 발사대 60대 
이상(*연초 40대에서 상향) 인도를 계획. 수출입 데이터상으로도 폴란드·루
마니아향 출하가 대규모로 진행 중이며 폴란드향 부속류 수출도 정상적으로 
이어지는 것으로 확인. 일회성 소멸과 고마진 수출 집중으로 수출 OPM은 
30% 내외로 회복할 것. 다만 국내는 정상 가동(2027년 상반기 목표) 전까
지 한 자릿수 초반 마진에 머물 것으로 전망. 
 중장기 수익성 우려도 크지 않다는 판단. 2027~2029년 매출 연 15% 성
장 가이던스는 유지됐고, 내년 폴란드 K9·천무 인도 물량은 모두 올해 대비 
증가할 계획. 폴란드 사업 이후 높아진 수출 마진 레벨은 후속 사업에서도 
유지될 전망. 수주는 중동 지정학 불안으로 지연되고 있으나 대공체계 등 문
의가 이어지고 있어, 2027년부터 중동이 수주의 중심이 될 것으로 기대. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 166만원을 유지함. 3Q26 지상방산 
쇼크는 국내 적자와 수출 물량 공백이 겹친 일시적 결과로, 수출 경쟁력 훼
손으로 보기 어려움. 실적 충격에 따른 주가 조정은 분할 매수 기회로 판단.  
 한화에어로스페이스의 주가는 4월 7일 사상 최고가 153.7만원에서 10월 
29일 76.0만원으로 -51% 조정 받은 상황. 12MF P/E 기준 14.3x, 27E 
P/E는 13.9x로 밸류에이션 관점에서도 매력적인 구간 진입. 그동안 3분기 
실적 우려 등으로 부진했다면, 이제는 실적 바텀아웃과 대형 수주 랠리 속 
다시 한 번 반등을 기록할 차례. 동사를 방산업종 Top-pick으로 유지함.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%) 
ㆍSTRONG BUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +50%이상 3% 
ㆍBUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +15%이상 ~ +50%미만 94% 
ㆍHOLD(중립) 추천기준일 종가대비 -10%이상 ∼ +15%미만 3% 
ㆍREDUCE(매도) 추천기준일 종가대비 -10%미만 0%

Document 294: AI・半導体で韓国景気は堅調も、「Ｋ字型」の様相が一段と鮮明に ～家計部門の低迷が李政権の重しとなる可能性～

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI・半導体で韓国景気は堅調も、「Ｋ字型」の様相が一段と鮮明に ～家計部門の低迷が李政権の重しとなる可能性～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-27

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 27 日

1/5 
AI・半導体で韓国景気は堅調も、「Ｋ字型」の様相が一段と鮮明に 
～家計部門の低迷が李政権の重しとなる可能性～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 韓国景気は、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要を追い風に、外需主導で堅調な推移が続いてい
る。 ７-９月の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前期比＋1.0％ （年率換算＋3.93％） 、 前年同期比＋3.4％
となり、2026 年９月までの累計成長率（＋3.6％）は中銀の８月時点の見通し（＋3.3％）を
上回った。メモリ半導体を中心とする財輸出に加え、半導体製造設備やデータセンター関連
の設備投資が成長を支え、2027 年も外需の堅調さが景気をけん引する展開が見込まれる。 
○ 一方、家計部門は力強さを欠いている。インフレ率は中銀目標の２％を上回って推移し、政
府の現金給付などの消費支援も効果が一巡したことから、個人消費は下振れた。成果給の恩
恵は一部の労働者にとどまり、業種間の賃金格差の拡大も懸念される。ソウルを中心に不動
産価格の上昇が続くなか、 家計債務を巡る金融リスクもくすぶっており、 中銀は10 月会合で
政策金利を据え置きつつも、追加利上げに含みを持たせた。KOSPI はピークから約４割下落
しており、金融市場の調整が家計に与える影響にも注意が必要である。 
○ 為替市場では、株価調整とは対照的に、ウォン相場が底堅く推移している。過去最高を更新
した貿易黒字に伴う実需と、中銀のタカ派姿勢が下支えとなっている。 
○ 政治面では、李在明政権の支持率が過去最低に近い水準にある。企業と家計、資産保有層と
それ以外の層との間で景気回復の恩恵に格差が生じる「Ｋ字型」の様相が強まっており、金
融市場の混乱が家計部門に波及すれば、政権の求心力が一段と低下する可能性がある。

【世界的なAI・半導体需要の旺盛さを追い風に、足元の韓国景気は堅調な推移が続いている】 
韓国では、 世界的なＡＩ （人工知能） ・ 半導体関連需要の旺盛な動きが景気の追い風となる展開が
続いている（注１）。韓国は、DRAM やNAND 型フラッシュメモリ、広帯域メモリ（ＨＢＭ）などメモ
リ半導体の大規模量産で世界トップクラスの競争力を有する。旺盛な半導体需要をけん引役に９月
の輸出額は過去最高額を更新するなど、輸出の勢いは強まっている。韓国はＧＤＰに占める輸出の

注１ 10 月１日付レポート「韓国､AI・半導体需要が景気に追い風も､政治への逆風は止まず」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666170.html）

比率が４割を上回るなど、 アジア太平洋
地域のなかでも外需依存度が相対的に
高い。こうしたなか、 ７-９月の実質ＧＤ
Ｐ成長率は前期比＋1.0％、年率換算ベ
ースでは＋3.93％となり、 景気の堅調さ

を上回る水準で推移している。 
世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大
を反映して財輸出が押し上げられるとともに（図２）、中国人観光客を中心とする来訪者数の増加
はサービス輸出を押し上げるなど、堅調な外需が景気をけん引する動きがみられる。さらに、 ＡＩ ・
半導体関連需要の旺盛さを追い風に、 半導体製造設備やデータセンター関連の投資が拡大するなど、
設備投資も景気を下支えしている。ま
た、政府消費の増加も成長に寄与した。
一方で、政府はイラン情勢の悪化を受け
た原油高の影響軽減を目的に、低所得者
層などを対象に１人当たり 10 万～60 万
ウォンの現金給付を実施したほか、 省エ
ネ家電の購入促進策 （キャッシュバック
の実施）などの消費支援に動いた。しか
し、足元ではその効果が一巡しており、
個人消費は下振れしている。さらに、前
期は在庫調整が進む動きが確認された
ものの、 当期は前期比年率ベースで＋0.32pt と試算されるなど、在庫の積み上がりを示唆する動き
もうかがえる。 
とはいえ、2026 年の９月までの累計ベースの経済成長率は＋3.6％となり、中銀が８月時点で示
した見通し （＋3.3％）を上回っている。 中銀は10 月会合において、2026 年の経済成長率が８月時
点の見通しを大きく上回る可能性を示唆したが、 足元の状況はそうした見方に沿った動きと捉えら
れる。 さらに、2027 年についても、 世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の動向に留意する必要があるも
のの、外需の堅調さが景気をけん引する展開が続く可能性が見込まれる。

【家計部門はインフレに直面、賃金上昇の恩恵は一部にとどまり、不動産高騰や株価調整も重し】 
外需は堅調な動きをみせる一方、対照的に個人消費は力強さを欠いている。背景には、2025 年後
半以降のインフレ率が中銀目標（２％）を上回る伸びで推移するなど、実質購買力に下押し圧力が
かかっていることがある。さらに、イラ
ン情勢の悪化を受けた 原油高を背景に
物価上昇圧力が残り、インフレ率は中銀
目標を上回る水準で推移している （図
３） 。これを受けて、政府は燃料価格の上
限規制 を約６ヵ月維持するなど物価対
策に動いている。しかし、足元では中東
情勢 の不透明 感を理由に 原油価格は高
止まりするなど、見通しが立たない状況
が続いている。一方で、半導体産業など
一部企業では、営業利益などの一定割合
（Ｎ％）を従業員の成果報酬の原資とする「Ｎ％成果給」が導入され、他産業にも導入を求める動
きが広がっている。 とはいえ、実際に高額な成果報酬の恩恵を受ける労働者は一部にとどまるなど、
業種間の賃金格差の拡大に繋がる懸念も高まっている。 
韓国ではここ数年、首都ソウルを中心とする不動産価格の高騰が社会問題化してきた。 政府は
2025 年以降に 様々な不動産高騰対策を
打ち出したものの、一連の対策では実需
層の資金調達が困難になる一方、規制対
象外の地域で不動産価格が上昇するな
ど「副作用」も顕在化した。また、政府
は超高額住宅や所有者が居住していな
い住宅に対する課税強化を目指したも
のの、中高所得者層などからの反発を受
けて一部見直しを余儀なくされた。さら
に、一連の政府主導による不動産対策に
対する不満は、６月に実施されたソウル
市長選で与党候補が敗北した一因との見方もある。とはいえ、 足元においてもソウルを中心とする
不動産価格は上昇が続いており （図４） 、住宅関連融資の増加が続くなど、 家計債務を巡る金融リス
クもくすぶっている。中銀は、10 月の定例会合で３会合ぶりに政策金利を据え置いたものの、 追加
利上げに含みを持たせる考えを示した（注２）。背景のひとつには、 不動産市場を巡るリスクがある

注２ 10 月22 日付レポート「韓国中銀、株価急落などで利上げ休止も、金融引き締めの長期化を示唆」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666810.html）

と考えられる。 
2025 年以降の韓国金融市場では、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の旺盛な動きを追い風に、主要
株価指数（KOSPI）は上昇基調を強めた。
さらに、 政府は2025 年末、 個人投資家が
保有する海外株式を売却して国内株式
に１年間投資した場合、一定の条件の下
で譲渡所得税を最大 100％控除する時限
措置を発表し、2026 年３月に法制化し
た。そのうえ、株価上昇を受けて個人投
資家の間でレバレッジ投資が活発化し
たことも、株価上昇の動きを後押しする
一助となった。しかし、構成銘柄全体の
時価総額に占める割合が５割前後を占
めるサムスン電子とＳＫハイニックスの２社の株価動向にKOSPI が左右されやすい構造にある。 こ
うしたなか、足元ではピークから約４割下落するなど、市場は大幅な調整に直面している（図５）
（注３）。こうした金融市場の不安定な動きも、家計部門に悪影響を与えている可能性がある。

【株価の大幅調整が続いているものの、貿易黒字と中銀のタカ派姿勢がウォン相場を下支え】 
金融市場においては、前述の通り半導体関連株を中心とする株価調整の動きに直面しているもの
の、 通貨ウォン相場は対照的に底堅い動きをみせている （図６） 。 背景には、世界的なＡＩ・半導体
関連需要の旺盛さが輸出を押し上げる
なか、足元の貿易黒字は過去最高を更新
するなど、ウォンに対する実需が生まれ
やすい環境にあることが挙げられる。ま
た、中銀が断続的な利上げを実施すると
ともに、先行きも追加利上げに含みを持
たせるなど「タカ派」姿勢を強めている
ことがある。さらに、 中銀の申総裁は10
月の定例会合後に行った記者会見にお
いて、「足元のウォンの対ドル相場は歴
史的に見れば低水準」 、 「実質実効ベース
でもウォンは依然割安」、「ウォン高は経済的不平等を是正する効果がある」と述べるなど、ウォン
高の経済的な利点を指摘したことも、ウォン相場を後押ししている可能性がある。

注３ 10 月15 日付レポート「韓国株が急落、AI・半導体好況の陰で市場の調整が深まる」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666600.html）

### Page 5

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
5/5
【金融市場の混乱や家計部門を取り巻く環境が李在明政権の行方を左右する可能性に要注意】
こうしたなか、李在明（イ・ジェミョン）政権の支持率は低水準で推移しており、足元では過去
最低に近い水準で推移している。 このところの支持率低下を巡っては、 若年層などで支持が弱まる
動きがみられるほか、 不動産政策や物価高への不満も影響していると考えられる。 こうした動きは、
政権が支持層をつなぎとめることの難しさを示唆している。足元の景気はいわゆる「Ｋ字型」の様
相を強めるとともに、 企業と家計、 資産保有層とそれ以外の層との間で景気回復の恩恵に格差が生
じており、その格差が一段と拡大する兆しもみられる。このため、先行きは実体経済の堅調さにも

かかわらず、李政権を取り巻く環境が厳しさを増す可能性も考えられる。与党・共に民主党を巡っ
ても、党内では政策運営などを巡る意見対立がみられるなど、火種を抱えている。金融市場の混乱
が家計部門に波及することで、 李政権の求心力をさらに弱める可能性がある点には注意が必要であ
る。

1WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUSTRALIA &
NEW ZEALAND
WEEKLY
Week beginning 2 November 2026
In this week’s edition:
Economic Insight: RBA to hike in November, as inflation remains too high.
The Week That Was: Cliff Notes: the source and persistence of inflation.
Focus on New Zealand: A lesson to be learned.

For the week ahead:
Australia: RBA policy decision, Cotality home value index, household spending indicator, goods trade
balance, dwelling approvals.
New Zealand: Q3 labour market surveys, building permits, LCI wage inflation.

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
We reaffirm our expectation  that the RBA Monetary Policy
Board will again increase the cash rate at the November
meeting. Today’s inflation data and Senate testimony are
the final pieces of information before the RBA Monetary
Policy Board (MPB) meets next week. Recent data and

communication have consistently pointed to the staff
recommending an increase in the cash rate. We expect a
majority of the external members to agree, though some
members might dissent.
Inflation is above target, and above what the RBA’s August
forecasts implied. Some upside risks identified in August have
partially materialised, particularly energy prices. The RBA is
especially worried that above-target inflation will become
embedded in people’s expectations and pricing decisions. It

will therefore deem that further policy tightening is needed.
The RBA has made it clear that it is down-weighting the recent
easing in the labour market, even though this was more than it
forecast. In today’s opening statement  to the Senate hearing,
the Governor said, “Despite the unemployment rate recently
rising a little more than we had expected, the labour market
seems a little tighter than we had previously judged. The
evidence for this is elevated underlying inflationary pressures.

That is, when we exclude some of the large volatile price
movements such as fuel we see inflationary pressure in areas
that reflect domestic demand for goods and services.” In the
Senate, the Governor also said that they were not worried
about the current pace of increase in unemployment.
Of course, this takes no account of the lagged relationship
between the labour market and inflation. The argument is that
if that measure of inflation last quarter was too high, it must

therefore be that the labour market is too tight. Nor does it
allow sufficiently for non-labour cost shocks, which business
surveys indicate are the real issue. But whether this judgement
by the RBA turns out to be right or not will not be revealed
for some time, certainly not soon enough to change its mind
before next week. And while some MPB members might have
a more optimistic view about labour supply trends than the
RBA staff, they are likely to be a minority.

The RBA is also relatively sanguine about the current
downturn in the housing market. The Governor and other
RBA officials are at pains to highlight that they do not target
housing prices; nor should they. The RBA considers that most,
though not all, of the current downswing is an expected effect
of tighter monetary policy.
RBA to hike in November, as inflation remains too high
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
The question then turns to the future beyond November. While

we still expect a hawkish tone in the RBA’s communication
until inflation has clearly turned down, further hikes beyond
next week are not currently our base case. Clearly, though, the
risks of another hike – most likely in February – have increased.
Our current forecast for Q4 inflation would seem to give them
cover for such a move. We note, however, that home-building
costs in the CPI have undershot expectations in the past couple
of months. This might imply a different view of the extent of

domestic demand pressures than the RBA currently believes.
However, a lot can happen in three months, as we have just
seen. The conflict in the Middle East  could escalate further,
lifting energy prices beyond our current base case. It could
also go the other way, though, if the US and Iran manage to
strike a post-midterm-election deal. And as their counterparts
in New Zealand highlighted today, one risk is that the global
economy weakens suddenly. In the post-meeting statement ,

the RBNZ Monetary Policy Committee said, “the world
economy could tip abruptly into a downturn causing weaker
inflation over the medium term.” This risk was something the
RBNZ MPC thought could crystallise in the next six weeks.
Whether on this timetable or something more drawn out, this
possibility does not appear to be on the RBA’s radar. Nor is it
our base case, though we have previously worried that the AI
financing boom could hit a wall quite suddenly.

“We reaffirm our expectation that the RBA
Monetary Policy Board will again increase the
cash rate at the November meeting"

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: the source and persistence of inflation
will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related
risks through 2027; although, in the absence of another shock,
we expect them to remain on hold.
The Q3 GDP report certainly supported Chair Warsh’s view

on the breadth of growth, the 3.4% annualised gain for GDP
benefitting from a 3.8% annualised increase in household
consumption, 12.7% growth in non-residential investment
as well as a robust increase in government spending.
Notably though, the contribution from business investment
was effectively offset by net exports’ drag given most of
the componentry related to AI infrastructure investment is
imported. This is not to say that US firms won’t benefit from

the investment once complete, but simply that much of the
initial return goes to the foreign businesses who provide the
necessary capital equipment.
On consumption, the monthly PCE data implies downside risks
remain for the consumer, the increase in nominal spending
seen in the month requiring dissaving and, on a real basis,
consumption flat in the month following a 0.5% gain in
August. Cost-of-living pressures and uncertainty over the
outlook is expected to see household consumption growth

average around trend over the coming year, albeit with
considerable volatility quarter to quarter. This should see PCE
inflation slow back towards its 2.0%yr medium-term target as
the FOMC desires, from 3.6%yr and 3.1%yr for the headline
and core measures respectively as of September.
Across the Atlantic, the ECB also left its key policy
rates unchanged in October. Of note from the meeting
communications, energy prices continue to pose upside risks

for inflation, but also downside risks for growth. "While the
full effects of the energy shock are still unfolding, underlying
inflation has remained broadly stable so far and wages do not
show a material response". "Financing conditions have [also]
tightened", "which is likely to weigh on inflation and growth".
In the press conference, President Lagarde noted that,
between the October and December meetings, a significant
amount of new economic information will be released,

allowing forecasts and risks assessments to be tested.
Based on the October meeting communications, we continue
to expect a rate hike at the December meeting but believe this
will be prove to be the last hike of the cycle. Note though, the
spectrum of risks Europe faces is broad and skewed towards
inflation in the near term, hence the Council’s guidance will
remain hawkish. President Lagarde also announced today that
she will step down as ECB President in February ahead of the

scheduled conclusion of her term next October.
In Australia this week, all eyes were on the September CPI .
Headline inflation rose 0.8% (4.3%yr) in the month, largely
owing to a 9.4% jump in fuel prices and a seasonal surge in
domestic and international holiday travel & accommodation.
The underlying inflation pulse also proved hotter than
anticipated for Q3 overall, up 1.0% (3.7%yr).
While some parts of the basket are seeing disinflation –
housing in particular looks to be cooling faster than expected

as builders lure sales with discounts – other components are
still showing persistence. Highlighting this is market services
ex volatiles, which the RBA watches closely as an indicator
of price pressures related to domestic capacity. In Q3, this
metric rose 1.2% (3.2%yr), and the monthly detail points
to momentum holding up – in line with our expectations for
another chunky read in Q4.
With inflation running well above the RBA’s August forecasts,

and some of the energy risks flagged then materialising, we
continue to expect the Monetary Policy Board will deliver a 25bp
rate hike to 4.85% in November. Beyond November, another rate
hike in February is a risk, but is not currently part of our base case.
Across the Tasman, the RBNZ this week surprised market
expectations for a 25bp rate hike, deciding instead to wait for
further information before taking the next step with policy. The
decision was made 4:3, with the Governor in the ‘on hold’ camp. A

25bp hike in December to 3.00% and further tightening in 2027
(Westpac NZ forecasts a peak of 4.00% in September) seems
probable; however, each decision will depend on fuel prices,
inflation expectations and evidence of second-round pricing
pressures, along with the evolution of global economic risks.
In the US, the FOMC also held rates steady  at its October
meeting. Based on prior comments by key members, the
decision was not a surprise, and neither were Governor Cook

and Cleveland Fed President Hammack’s dissents in favour
of a 25bp hike. The Committee remains positive on economic
activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding at
a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in September). Chair
Warsh also subsequently emphasised the breadth of economic
growth outside of tech-related investment (evinced by Q3
GDP the day after; see below).
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in
the statement. However, inflation “remains elevated” and the

FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable
return of annual inflation to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target.
This guidance and the “accumulation of more information will
help the Committee determine the policy stance” suggests
another 25bp hike is likely in December. Thereafter, the FOMC
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
A lesson to be learned
The RBNZ delivered the biggest shock to markets outside of
a crisis period in over 20 years this week by leaving the OCR
unchanged. Markets had priced around a 98% probability of
a 25bp increase, and all major domestic analysts concurred.

After the CPI data the week earlier, we belatedly shifted our
call to a hike despite plenty of good arguments in favour of
the RBNZ sticking to its plan to pause. With the benefit of
hindsight, too much weight was put on market pricing and
short-term inflation drivers and not enough on the medium
term. That’s an important lesson to be learned.
The highlight of an action-packed week of central banking news
was the unexpected decision of the RBNZ to leave the OCR at

2.75%, despite near unanimous analyst expectations and market
pricing in favour of a 25bp hike. We had shifted sides from a
hold to a hike just a week earlier, in the wake of a CPI outcome
that was weaker than RBNZ expectations on the surface, but
with some evidence of firm core inflation undertones.
While we clearly cannot claim to have gotten this call right,
we always thought that the outcome was not the “done deal”
that markets had priced in. For example, in our preview of the

meeting we noted:
“We still think a more measured tightening approach is
appropriate from here. Raising the OCR at every meeting
until something ‘breaks’ could be a risky strategy if downside
growth concerns crystalise.”
And we saw a vote on the decision as very possible:
“A vote seems very possible as differences in view will likely
remain among the MPC about how much the medium-term
inflation outlook has really shifted in recent weeks.”
The 4:3 vote that was ultimately required to bring the MPC

to a decision is a testament to just how finely balanced the
decision was. We had thought that the hike was perhaps
around a 60-70% probability whereas the Record of Meeting
implies something more like a 50:50 call.
In the final analysis, the RBNZ has merely followed through on
the pause it signalled for this meeting back in the September
Monetary Policy Statement. All MPC members continue to see
a case for a higher OCR in time, but there are different views

around the timing of those increases. Governor Breman noted
that further interest rate increases remain likely in coming
meetings but are conditional on the whether the outlook
evolves in line with expectations. Indeed, the Governor noted
in later comments to press that a December hike was a “high”
probability and that the outlook remains for OCR “hikes” as the
base case. This suggests that the RBNZ’s view on the terminal
OCR hasn’t really shifted down from the 3.25% level presented

back in September. And it may be reasonable to speculate,
subject to data evolving as the RBNZ expects, that a modest
upgrade in those forward OCR forecasts could be in the offing.
So how did we all get this so wrong? This outcome was a real
shock given the historical record shows that over the history of
the OCR the market has without exception correctly predicted
a 25bp hike when the implied probability built into markets
was as high as it was. Clearly some recalibration of the model

is required. We can identify two key areas where lessons
should be learned: too much weight on market pricing; and not
enough on drivers of medium-term inflation.
Market pricing did not adequately reflect the
MPC’s desire to shift strategy .
Firstly, and most importantly, we put too much weight on
market pricing and too little on the RBNZ’s clearly stated
strategy of preferring a pause at the October meeting to
assess the impact of interest rate increases already done. In our

Preview we incorrectly surmised that silence equated to assent
and that market pricing of a hike would make an OCR hike the
less disruptive option – especially given the sense that a hike
to 3% was still likely in December as implied by the RBNZ’s
September forecasts. From a macroeconomic perspective,
raising rates six weeks early is of little consequence.
This logic, as sound as it was, was wrong, as market pricing did
not reflect a large enough change in the medium-term inflation

outlook to justify a change in RBNZ strategy. Markets at least
partially held the view, based on a long history of revealed
RBNZ preference, that the RBNZ keeps moving the OCR in the
same direction until confronted with compelling evidence that
a halt to the cycle is in prospect. That operating approach may
be under review now.
It’s not uncommon for other central banks to take breaks
while amid an interest rate cycle to gather information on
the impact of work already done before moving forward.

This week’s pause by the FOMC in the face of a fierce set of
monthly inflation readings in the last month is an example.
Pausing does not need to imply stopping or reversing. Indeed,
taking a more measured approach could prove to be solid
risk management – as we identified in our MPR preview note.
Risk management concerns probably should have greater
prominence in this cycle given we are seeing a measure of
Kelly Eckhold
Chief Economist NZ

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
supply side cost shocks that might not be persistent. The
Statement of Record contains a wealth of material related to
these sorts of uncertainties and concerns. Markets were not
listening to these concerns, however.
An insufficient focus on drivers of medium-term

inflation
Markets were not listening to the alarm bells because there
was a heavy focus on factors that may be more relevant to the
short-term inflation outlook than medium-term inflation. Top
of mind were the rise in energy prices and fall in the exchange
rate. Both factors look set to significantly boost the short-
term inflation outlook – for example we currently forecast
December quarter annual inflation of 4.3%, with upside risks
from there if fuel prices do not fall over the balance of the

quarter. However, our analysis of the data surprises suggested
no significant shift to the implied RBNZ OCR track one year
ahead. Upward pressure on the future OCR projection from
the lower exchange rate and stronger GDP was balanced
by higher long term interest rates and the lower Q3 CPI
outcome. Higher energy prices were not seen as a key factor
as they don’t impact medium term inflation unless inflation
expectations are dislodged (no evidence was found of that

since September) or if the increase in energy prices is viewed
as persistent (futures markets continue to imply lower forward
energy prices, although MPC hawks clearly see risks of
persistent inflation coming from supply shocks).
The RBNZ’s communications at the MPR and in its aftermath
are directing markets to pay more attention to medium-term
inflation drivers in the future. Hence the references to the
strength of demand and inflation expectations. The risk to

the RBNZ’s inflation forecasts are to the upside, and hence
we might reasonably expect some upgrade of the OCR profile
when we see new forecasts in December. But it’s not a done
deal by any means as data and events between now and then
will dictate what stance the RBNZ takes. This week’s business
and consumer confidence reports do not seem to present many
smoking guns of imminent second-round inflation pressures,
even though business and consumer sentiment seems to be

taking the higher oil price in its stride.
Is core inflation high enough yet to shift the
strategy?
Another interesting point made by the Governor was that the
markets (and analysts) misinterpreted how the September
quarter CPI was read by the RBNZ, which evidently took
greater comfort from the outcome. Governor Breman has noted
she wished the RBNZ has protocols in place to communicate
with markets the RBNZ’s view on that CPI data given that it
seems to have been a factor that figured importantly in at least

some MPC member’s thinking. Headline inflation was lower
than the RBNZ expected and importantly the core inflation
measures, that we had thought might have been of concern to
the MPC, do not yet seem to have been at a level sufficient to
shift the RBNZ from its pause-then-hike strategy. We remain
sceptical that a 3.25% OCR will prove sufficient to bring core
inflation down significantly. But core inflation appears to need
to lift for the MPC to become more agitated. It likely will lift,

but we won’t get more data on that until January 2027.
December hike likely but not a done deal
We expect a further 25bp hike at this year’s final meeting on 9
December and then 25bp increases in the February and March
2027 meetings, followed by a slower pace of increases at the
June and September Monetary Policy Statement meetings.
Our forecast of a peak OCR of 4% from September 2027 has
remained unchanged throughout, as has our assessment of a
3.75% neutral OCR.

That said, in the current uncertain environment, it goes without
saying that the evolution of monetary policy will depend on
how both global events and key data evolve. Therefore, as the
RBNZ noted itself, the exact timing of the tightening profile
is highly uncertain and even the tightening we forecast at the
December meeting should not be regarded as a done deal.
Looking ahead, the ‘hardest’ data to come will be the Q3
labour market surveys where we anticipate a small fall in

the unemployment rate to 5.5% but still moderate wage
pressures. The December quarter RBNZ inflation expectations
surveys will be of significant interest (17, 20 and 24
November) for MPC members looking for persistent inflation
impacts. The usual run of monthly activity and selected prices
indicators will be useful in narrowing down Q4 growth and
inflation momentum. And finally, the MPC’s focus on external
risks will imply keeping an eye on global fixed income, equity

and credit markets for signs of imminent calamity.
Finally, November 7 is the general election. A close race is
envisaged that could impact on the RBNZ’s mandate. We
will be offering client briefings on Monday 9 November and
Tuesday 10 November to outline the immediate implications
of the election outcome for markets and the economy. Contact
your relationship manager for details.

AUS: RBA Policy Decision (%)
Nov 3, Last: 4.60, Westpac f/c: 4.85
Market f/c: 4.85, Range: 4.60 to 4.85
We reaffirm our expectation that the RBA Monetary Policy
Board (MPB) will decide to raise the cash rate by 25bps to
4.85% at the November meeting.

Inflation is above target, and above what the RBA’s August
forecasts implied. Some upside risks identified in August have
partially materialised, particularly energy prices. The RBA is
especially worried that above-target inflation will become
embedded in people’s expectations and pricing decisions.
Recent data and communication have consistently pointed to
the RBA staff recommending an increase in the cash rate. We
expect a majority of the external board members to agree,

though some members might dissent.
AUS: Oct Cotality Home Value Index (%mth)
Nov 2, Last: –1.1, Westpac f/c: –1.3
The Cotality home value index – covering all of Australia – fell
1.1% in Sep with downward revisions shaving a further 0.4%
off prices. The correction has now seen prices decline 5.2%
from their March peak, annual growth stalling flat. Cotality
reported 97% of capital city suburbs experienced falls over the
three months to September.
The October update is set to be another weak one with an

added hit coming from the RBA’s interest rate rise in late
September. The daily measure (covering the five major capital
cities rather than all of Australia) is tracking a 1.5% decline vs
1.3% in September. The detail points to slightly bigger falls
in Sydney, Brisbane and Perth with Melbourne continuing
to record more moderate declines. Note that seasonality is
usually a slight positive in the October month.
AUS: Sep Dwelling Approvals (%mth)
Nov 2, Last: –6.1, Westpac f/c: –7.0

Market f/c: 2.0, Range: –7.0 to 5.0
Dwelling approvals fell 6.1%mth in August, with the annual
pace easing slightly to 10.3%yr. Weakness centred on unit
approvals with private detached house approvals actually
posting a solid rebound from a July month fall, touching a new
cycle high (up 18.4%yr).
We expect the September update to show a much clearer
weakening. New construction is coming under pressure
from multiple fronts: interest rate rises; a sharp downturn in

established markets; and rising costs – with a great deal of
uncertainty around each of these. New home sales have fell
sharply through the September quarter and we expect to
see detached house approvals show something similar. Unit
approvals are coming off a weak read and should be more
stable. Total approvals are expected to print 7% lower.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Sep Goods Trade Balance ($bn)
Nov 5, Last: 0.5, Westpac f/c: 2.0
Market f/c: 1.0, Range: –1.0 to 2.5
Australia’s goods trade surplus narrowed to $0.5bn in August.
Recently, the trade balance has been oscillating between
small deficits and surpluses with significant volatility in the

underlying trade mix.
Swings in non-monetary gold, fuel and cars have all played
a large role, but the most eye-catching result from Aug was
the 79% surge in automatic data processing equipment (ADP)
imports, which captures the investment upswing related to
data centres.
For September, we expect an increase in the surplus as the
ADP import surge unwinds, though we are likely in store for
ongoing monthly volatility in trade for some time.
NZ: Sep Building Consents (%mth)

Nov 3, Last: +5.6%, Westpac f/c: Flat
Annual residential building consent numbers are currently at
a three year high, and we expect that they will remain firm in
the September update. However, monthly consent numbers
are showing signs of peaking, with falls in three of the last four
months. We’re watching to see if that trend has continued,
particularly given the ongoing softness in the housing
market.  In the non-residential space, we’ve seen softness

in the number of retail and office developments, but greater
resilience in the industrial and storage segment.
AUS: Sep Household Spending Indicator (%mth)
Nov 4, Last: flat, Westpac f/c: –0.5
Market f/c: 0.2, Range: –0.5 to 0.8
The household spending indicator was unchanged in August,
annual growth easing to 6.8%yr. The detail showed a modest
rotation away from discretionary categories towards non-
discretionary.
Our Westpac-DataX Card Tracker  showed a decent rise in

the September month although new vehicle sales (which are
not covered by the tracker but are in scope for the spending
indicator) look to have posted a decline. Overall we expect
the indicator to show a 0.5% decline in the September month.
Note that the update with also include Q3 estimates of real
spending – we expect this to show a 0.6%qtr gain with prices
up 1.2%qtr and nominal spend up 1.8%qtr.
